===== /tmp/eear-ia05-v4-final/matriz.py =====
import os,sys,json,subprocess,time,hashlib
from pathlib import Path
repo=Path('/tmp/eear-ia05-encerramento');out=Path('/tmp/eear-ia05-v4-final');g=sys.argv[1]
env=dict(os.environ,EEAR_REPO_REPROVADO_IA05='/tmp/eear-audit2-runtime',EEAR_REPO_CLIENTE_V3='/tmp/eear-ia05-verificado',NODE_PATH='/tmp/eear-audit-deps/node_modules',PLAYWRIGHT_BROWSERS_PATH='/tmp/eear-audit-browsers',EEAR_FIREBASE_SDK='/tmp/eear-audit-deps/node_modules/firebase',EEAR_FIREBASE_CLI='/tmp/eear-audit-deps/node_modules/.bin/firebase',EEAR_REPO_ALVO=str(repo),EEAR_REPO_LEGADO='/tmp/eear-2f6cad6',EEAR_REPO_REPROVADO='/tmp/eear-audit-a35-source')
linhas=subprocess.check_output(['git','ls-tree','-r','HEAD'],cwd=repo,text=True).splitlines();entradas=[]
for linha in linhas:
 meta,nome=linha.split('\t',1);modo,tipo,sha=meta.split()
 if tipo=='blob' and (nome.startswith(('curso/','web/','testes/','.github/workflows/')) or nome in ['firebase.json','.firebaserc']):entradas.append(dict(path=nome,sha=sha))
def conferir():
 for x in entradas:

import os,subprocess,json,time,hashlib
from pathlib import Path
r=Path('/tmp/eear-ia05-encerramento');out=Path('/tmp/eear-ia05-v4-final');(out/'null.json').write_text('null')
env=dict(os.environ,NODE_PATH='/tmp/eear-audit-deps/node_modules',PLAYWRIGHT_BROWSERS_PATH='/tmp/eear-audit-browsers',EEAR_FIREBASE_CONFIG=str(out/'null.json'))
h=hashlib.sha256((r/'curso/app.js').read_bytes()).hexdigest();result=[]
def run(n,args,cwd=r,expected=0,extra={}):
 start=time.time()
 with (out/(n+'.log')).open('w') as f:p=subprocess.run(args,cwd=cwd,env=dict(env,**extra),stdout=f,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
 assert hashlib.sha256((r/'curso/app.js').read_bytes()).hexdigest()==h
 x=dict(nome=n,comando=args,cwd=str(cwd),exit=p.returncode,exitEsperado=expected,segundos=round(time.time()-start,2),sha256=h);result.append(x);(out/'final-extras-resultados.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2));print(json.dumps(x),flush=True)

run('validar-app',['python3','-I','web/verificar_publicacao.py','verificar',str(out/'app'),'--app'])
for destino in ['site','app']:
 assert (out/destino/'app.js').read_bytes()==(r/'curso/app.js').read_bytes()
run('build-origem-d165',['python3','web/build.py',str(out/'site-d165')],Path('/tmp/eear-legado-encerramento'))

===== /tmp/eear-ia05-v4-final/executar.py =====
import subprocess,time,json
from pathlib import Path
o=Path('/tmp/eear-ia05-v4-final');ps={}
for g in ['firebase','bloqueadores','local','extras']:
 f=(o/('progresso-'+g+'.log')).open('w');a=['python3',str(o/('extras.py' if g=='extras' else 'matriz.py'))]+([] if g=='extras' else [g]);ps[g]=(subprocess.Popen(a,stdout=f,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT),f)
while ps:
 for g,(p,f) in list(ps.items()):
  if p.poll() is not None:print(g+': controlador terminou com '+str(p.returncode),flush=True);f.close();del ps[g]
 print('Matriz integral em andamento: '+', '.join(ps),flush=True)
 if ps:time.sleep(20)
r=[]
for g,total in [('firebase',11),('bloqueadores',2),('local',19),('extras',10)]:
 p=o/('final-'+g+'-resultados.json');a=json.loads(p.read_text()) if p.exists() else [];falhas=[x['nome'] for x in a if x['exit']!=x.get('exitEsperado',0)];r.append(dict(grupo=g,esperados=total,executados=len(a),falhas=falhas));print(json.dumps(r[-1]),flush=True)
(o/'matriz-integral-resumo.json').write_text(json.dumps(r,indent=2))
raise SystemExit(1 if any(x['esperados']!=x['executados'] or x['falhas'] for x in r) else 0)


===== /tmp/eear-ia05-v4-final/firebase-corrigido.py =====
import os,time,subprocess,json,hashlib
from pathlib import Path
repo=Path('/tmp/eear-ia05-fixtures-final');out=Path('/tmp/eear-ia05-v4-final')
while True:
 ativo=False
 for d in Path('/proc').iterdir():
  if d.name.isdigit():
   try:
    cmd=(d/'cmdline').read_bytes().replace(b'\0',b' ')
    if b'python3 /tmp/eear-ia05-v4-final/matriz.py firebase' in cmd and b'firebase-corrigido.py' not in cmd:ativo=True
   except OSError:pass
 if not ativo:break
 print('Aguardando portas da matriz de persistência; sem executar duas suítes no mesmo emulador.',flush=True);time.sleep(20)
entradas=[]
for l in subprocess.check_output(['git','ls-tree','-r','HEAD'],cwd=repo,text=True).splitlines():
 m,n=l.split('\t',1);_,tipo,sha=m.split()
 if tipo=='blob' and (n.startswith(('curso/','web/','testes/','.github/workflows/')) or n in ['firebase.json','.firebaserc']):entradas.append((n,sha))
def conferir():
 for n,sha in entradas:
  b=(repo/n).read_bytes();assert hashlib.sha1(b'blob '+str(len(b)).encode()+b'\0'+b).hexdigest()==sha,n
 return hashlib.sha256((repo/'curso/app.js').read_bytes()).hexdigest()
h=conferir();assert h=='115d8af1fef7b82da3a006103512bfd6ac0897b5b43ba8eaf3b3962ed1c0cf9b'
env=dict(os.environ,NODE_PATH='/tmp/eear-audit-deps/node_modules',PLAYWRIGHT_BROWSERS_PATH='/tmp/eear-audit-browsers',EEAR_FIREBASE_SDK='/tmp/eear-audit-deps/node_modules/firebase',EEAR_FIREBASE_CLI='/tmp/eear-audit-deps/node_modules/.bin/firebase')
start=time.time()
with (out/'firebase-53-corrigido.log').open('w') as f:p=subprocess.run(['node','testes/firebase-emulador.test.js'],cwd=repo,env=env,stdout=f,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
depois=conferir();r=dict(nome='firebase-53',comando='node testes/firebase-emulador.test.js',variaveis={},alvo=str(repo),runtime='5b3f9d4cfec2a5597d57aab543d6358e4024a85a',shaTestes=subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],cwd=repo,text=True).strip(),exit=p.returncode,segundos=round(time.time()-start,2),sha256Antes=h,sha256Depois=depois,arquivosConferidos=len(entradas));(out/'firebase-corrigido-resultados.json').write_text(json.dumps(r,indent=2));print(json.dumps(r),flush=True)
raise SystemExit(p.returncode)

===== /tmp/eear-ia05-v4/direcionados.py =====
import subprocess,os,json,time
from pathlib import Path
repo=Path('/workspace/eear-dev');out=Path('/tmp/eear-ia05-v4');env=dict(os.environ,NODE_PATH='/tmp/eear-audit-deps/node_modules',PLAYWRIGHT_BROWSERS_PATH='/tmp/eear-audit-browsers',EEAR_FIREBASE_SDK='/tmp/eear-audit-deps/node_modules/firebase',EEAR_FIREBASE_CLI='/tmp/eear-audit-deps/node_modules/.bin/firebase',EEAR_REPO_REPROVADO_IA05='/tmp/eear-audit2-runtime',EEAR_REPO_CLIENTE_V3='/tmp/eear-ia05-verificado')
r=[]
for n,a,extra,e in [('reset-anterior',['node','testes/importacoes-reset-offline.test.js'],{'EEAR_REPO_ALVO':'/tmp/eear-ia05-verificado'},1),('reset-v4',['node','testes/importacoes-reset-offline.test.js'],{},0),('clientes-v2-v3',['node','testes/importacoes-cliente-anterior.test.js'],{},0),('exclusao-ia01',['node','testes/a35-importacao-exclusao.test.js'],{},0),('ciclo',['node','testes/importacoes-ciclo.test.js'],{},0)]:
 start=time.time()
 with (out/(n+'.log')).open('w') as f:p=subprocess.run(a,cwd=repo,env=dict(env,**extra),stdout=f,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
 x=dict(nome=n,exit=p.returncode,esperado=e,segundos=round(time.time()-start,2));r.append(x);(out/'direcionados-resultados.json').write_text(json.dumps(r,indent=2));print(json.dumps(x),flush=True)
raise SystemExit(1 if any(x['exit']!=x['esperado'] for x in r) else 0)
